+std::vector<double> gatherGlobalResult(const std::vector<double>& localField,
+                                       const Domain& domain) {
+    if (domain.ownedSize() > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        if (domain.rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "Error: local result exceeds MPI_Gatherv count limit\n");
+        MPI_Abort(domain.comm, 2);
+    }
+    const int localCount = static_cast<int>(domain.ownedSize());
+    std::vector<double> packed(domain.ownedSize());
+    size_t p = 0;
+    for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain.local[2]; ++z)
+        for (size_t y = 1; y <= domain.local[1]; ++y) {
+            std::copy_n(localField.data() + domain.index(1, y, z), domain.local[0],
+                        packed.data() + p);
+            p += domain.local[0];
+        }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (domain.rank == 0) {
+        counts.resize(domain.processCount);
+        displacements.resize(domain.processCount);
+    }
+    MPI_Gather(&localCount, 1, MPI_INT, counts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, 0, domain.comm);
+
+    std::vector<double> rankOrdered;
+    if (domain.rank == 0) {
+        long long total = 0;
+        for (int r = 0; r < domain.processCount; ++r) {
+            if (total > std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) {
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Error: global result exceeds MPI_Gatherv displacement limit\n");
+                MPI_Abort(domain.comm, 2);
+            }
+            displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(total);
+            total += counts[r];
+        }
+        if (total > std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Error: global result exceeds MPI_Gatherv count limit\n");
+            MPI_Abort(domain.comm, 2);
+        }
+        rankOrdered.resize(static_cast<size_t>(total));
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(packed.data(), localCount, MPI_DOUBLE, rankOrdered.data(), counts.data(),
+                displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, domain.comm);
+
+    std::vector<double> globalField;
+    if (domain.rank == 0) {
+        globalField.resize(domain.global[0] * domain.global[1] * domain.global[2]);
+        for (int r = 0; r < domain.processCount; ++r) {
+            std::array<int, 3> coordinates{};
+            MPI_Cart_coords(domain.comm, r, 3, coordinates.data());
+            const Extent ex = splitExtent(domain.global[0], domain.dims[0], coordinates[0]);
+            const Extent ey = splitExtent(domain.global[1], domain.dims[1], coordinates[1]);
+            const Extent ez = splitExtent(domain.global[2], domain.dims[2], coordinates[2]);
+            size_t source = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[r]);
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < ez.size; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < ey.size; ++y) {
+                    const size_t destination =
+                        idx3(ex.offset, ey.offset + y, ez.offset + z, domain.global[0],
+                             domain.global[1]);
+                    std::copy_n(rankOrdered.data() + source, ex.size,
+                                globalField.data() + destination);
+                    source += ex.size;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return globalField;
+}

    30	Extent splitExtent(const size_t globalSize, const int partitions, const int coordinate) {
    31	    const size_t count = globalSize / static_cast<size_t>(partitions);
    32	    const size_t remainder = globalSize % static_cast<size_t>(partitions);

    37	}
    38	
    39	// Choose a process grid that minimizes the slowest rank's work and then the
    40	// total inter-rank interface area.  The final tie-break favors Z/Y cuts because
    41	// their faces are cheaper to pack from the X-major storage layout.
    42	std::array<int, 3> chooseProcessGrid(const int processCount, const size_t nx,
    43	                                     const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    44	    std::array<int, 3> best = {0, 0, 0};
    45	    long double bestScore = std::numeric_limits<long double>::infinity();
    46	
    47	    for (int px = 1; px <= processCount && static_cast<size_t>(px) <= nx; ++px) {
    48	        if (processCount % px != 0) continue;
    49	        const int yz = processCount / px;
    50	        for (int py = 1; py <= yz && static_cast<size_t>(py) <= ny; ++py) {
    51	            if (yz % py != 0) continue;
    52	            const int pz = yz / py;
    53	            if (static_cast<size_t>(pz) > nz) continue;
    54	
    55	            const long double maxVolume =
    56	                static_cast<long double>((nx + px - 1) / px) *
    57	                static_cast<long double>((ny + py - 1) / py) *
    58	                static_cast<long double>((nz + pz - 1) / pz);
    59	            const long double interfaces =
    60	                static_cast<long double>(px - 1) * ny * nz +
    61	                static_cast<long double>(py - 1) * nx * nz +
    62	                static_cast<long double>(pz - 1) * nx * ny;
    63	            const long double score = maxVolume + interfaces;
    64	
    65	            const bool betterTie = score == bestScore &&
    66	                (pz > best[2] || (pz == best[2] && py > best[1]));
    67	            if (score < bestScore || betterTie) {
    68	                bestScore = score;
    69	                best = {px, py, pz};
    70	            }
    71	        }
    72	    }
    73	    return best;
    74	}
    75	
    76	struct Domain {
    77	    MPI_Comm comm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
    78	    int rank = 0;
    79	    int processCount = 1;
    80	    std::array<int, 3> dims{};
    81	    std::array<int, 3> coords{};
    82	    std::array<int, 6> neighbor{};
    83	    std::array<size_t, 3> global{};
    84	    std::array<size_t, 3> local{};
    85	    std::array<size_t, 3> offset{};
    86	    size_t rowStride = 0;
    87	    size_t planeStride = 0;
    88	
    89	    Domain(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) : global{nx, ny, nz} {
    90	        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &processCount);
    91	        dims = chooseProcessGrid(processCount, nx, ny, nz);
    92	        if (dims[0] == 0) {
    93	            if (processCount > 0) {
    94	                int worldRank = 0;
    95	                MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank);
    96	                if (worldRank == 0) {
    97	                    std::fprintf(stderr,
    98	                                 "Error: the %zu x %zu x %zu grid cannot be split among %d nonempty MPI ranks\n",
    99	                                 nx, ny, nz, processCount);
   100	                }
   101	            }
   102	            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
   103	        }
   104	
   105	        const int periods[3] = {0, 0, 0};
   106	        MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 3, dims.data(), periods, 0, &comm);
   107	        MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
   108	        MPI_Cart_coords(comm, rank, 3, coords.data());
   109	        MPI_Cart_shift(comm, 0, 1, &neighbor[X_MINUS], &neighbor[X_PLUS]);
   110	        MPI_Cart_shift(comm, 1, 1, &neighbor[Y_MINUS], &neighbor[Y_PLUS]);
   111	        MPI_Cart_shift(comm, 2, 1, &neighbor[Z_MINUS], &neighbor[Z_PLUS]);
   112	
   113	        for (int d = 0; d < 3; ++d) {
   114	            const Extent extent = splitExtent(global[d], dims[d], coords[d]);
   115	            local[d] = extent.size;
   116	            offset[d] = extent.offset;
   117	        }
   118	        rowStride = local[0] + 2;
   119	        planeStride = rowStride * (local[1] + 2);
   120	    }
   121	
   122	    ~Domain() {
   123	        if (comm != MPI_COMM_NULL) MPI_Comm_free(&comm);
   124	    }
   125	
   126	    Domain(const Domain&) = delete;
   127	    Domain& operator=(const Domain&) = delete;
   128	
   129	    size_t index(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) const noexcept {
   130	        return z * planeStride + y * rowStride + x;
   131	    }
   132	
   133	    size_t allocatedSize() const noexcept { return planeStride * (local[2] + 2); }
   134	    size_t ownedSize() const noexcept { return local[0] * local[1] * local[2]; }
   135	};
   136	
   137	class HaloExchange {
   138	  public:
   139	    explicit HaloExchange(const Domain& domain) : domain_(domain) {
   140	        const size_t xFace = domain_.local[1] * domain_.local[2];
   141	        const size_t yFace = domain_.local[0] * domain_.local[2];
   142	        const size_t zFace = domain_.local[0] * domain_.local[1];
   143	        const std::array<size_t, 6> sizes = {xFace, xFace, yFace, yFace, zFace, zFace};
   144	
   145	        for (int face = 0; face < 6; ++face) {
   146	            if (sizes[face] > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
   147	                if (domain_.rank == 0) {
   148	                    std::fprintf(stderr, "Error: an MPI halo exceeds the implementation's count limit\n");
   149	                }
   150	                MPI_Abort(domain_.comm, 2);
   151	            }
   152	            send_[face].resize(sizes[face]);
   153	            receive_[face].resize(sizes[face]);
   154	            if (domain_.neighbor[face] == MPI_PROC_NULL) continue;
   155	
   156	            MPI_Request receiveRequest;
   157	            MPI_Request sendRequest;
   158	            MPI_Recv_init(receive_[face].data(), static_cast<int>(sizes[face]), MPI_DOUBLE,
   159	                          domain_.neighbor[face], 100 + oppositeFace(face), domain_.comm,
   160	                          &receiveRequest);
   161	            MPI_Send_init(send_[face].data(), static_cast<int>(sizes[face]), MPI_DOUBLE,
   162	                          domain_.neighbor[face], 100 + face, domain_.comm, &sendRequest);
   163	            requests_.push_back(receiveRequest);
   164	            requests_.push_back(sendRequest);
   165	        }
   166	    }
   167	
   168	    ~HaloExchange() {
   169	        for (MPI_Request& request : requests_) MPI_Request_free(&request);
   170	    }
   171	
   172	    HaloExchange(const HaloExchange&) = delete;
   173	    HaloExchange& operator=(const HaloExchange&) = delete;
   174	
   175	    void begin(std::vector<double>& field) {
   176	        packOrClampX(field);
   177	        packOrClampY(field);
   178	        packOrClampZ(field);
   179	        if (!requests_.empty()) {
   180	            MPI_Startall(static_cast<int>(requests_.size()), requests_.data());
   181	        }
   182	    }
   183	
   184	    void end(std::vector<double>& field) {
   185	        if (!requests_.empty()) {
   186	            MPI_Waitall(static_cast<int>(requests_.size()), requests_.data(), MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
   187	        }
   188	        unpackX(field);
   189	        unpackY(field);
   190	        unpackZ(field);
   191	    }
   192	
   193	  private:
   194	    const Domain& domain_;
   195	    std::array<std::vector<double>, 6> send_;
   196	    std::array<std::vector<double>, 6> receive_;
   197	    std::vector<MPI_Request> requests_;
   198	
   199	    void packOrClampX(std::vector<double>& field) {
   200	        size_t p = 0;
   201	        for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain_.local[2]; ++z) {
   202	            for (size_t y = 1; y <= domain_.local[1]; ++y) {
   203	                const size_t base = domain_.index(0, y, z);
   204	                if (domain_.neighbor[X_MINUS] == MPI_PROC_NULL) field[base] = field[base + 1];
   205	                else send_[X_MINUS][p] = field[base + 1];
   206	                if (domain_.neighbor[X_PLUS] == MPI_PROC_NULL)
   207	                    field[base + domain_.local[0] + 1] = field[base + domain_.local[0]];
   208	                else
   209	                    send_[X_PLUS][p] = field[base + domain_.local[0]];
   210	                ++p;
   211	            }
   212	        }
   213	    }
   214	
   215	    void packOrClampY(std::vector<double>& field) {
   216	        size_t p = 0;
   217	        for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain_.local[2]; ++z) {
   218	            const size_t lower = domain_.index(1, 0, z);
   219	            const size_t upper = domain_.index(1, domain_.local[1], z);
   220	            if (domain_.neighbor[Y_MINUS] == MPI_PROC_NULL)
   220	            if (domain_.neighbor[Y_MINUS] == MPI_PROC_NULL)
   221	                std::copy_n(field.data() + lower + domain_.rowStride, domain_.local[0],
   222	                            field.data() + lower);
   223	            else {
   224	                std::copy_n(field.data() + lower + domain_.rowStride, domain_.local[0],
   225	                            send_[Y_MINUS].data() + p);
   226	            }
   227	            if (domain_.neighbor[Y_PLUS] == MPI_PROC_NULL)
   228	                std::copy_n(field.data() + upper, domain_.local[0],
   229	                            field.data() + upper + domain_.rowStride);
   230	            else
   231	                std::copy_n(field.data() + upper, domain_.local[0], send_[Y_PLUS].data() + p);
   232	            p += domain_.local[0];
   233	        }
   234	    }
   235	
   236	    void packOrClampZ(std::vector<double>& field) {
   237	        size_t p = 0;
   238	        for (size_t y = 1; y <= domain_.local[1]; ++y) {
   239	            const size_t lower = domain_.index(1, y, 0);
   240	            const size_t upper = domain_.index(1, y, domain_.local[2]);
   241	            if (domain_.neighbor[Z_MINUS] == MPI_PROC_NULL)
   242	                std::copy_n(field.data() + lower + domain_.planeStride, domain_.local[0],
   243	                            field.data() + lower);
   244	            else
   245	                std::copy_n(field.data() + lower + domain_.planeStride, domain_.local[0],
   246	                            send_[Z_MINUS].data() + p);
   247	            if (domain_.neighbor[Z_PLUS] == MPI_PROC_NULL)
   248	                std::copy_n(field.data() + upper, domain_.local[0],
   249	                            field.data() + upper + domain_.planeStride);
   250	            else
   251	                std::copy_n(field.data() + upper, domain_.local[0], send_[Z_PLUS].data() + p);
   252	            p += domain_.local[0];
   253	        }
   254	    }
   255	
   256	    void unpackX(std::vector<double>& field) {
   257	        size_t p = 0;
   258	        for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain_.local[2]; ++z) {
   259	            for (size_t y = 1; y <= domain_.local[1]; ++y) {
   260	                const size_t base = domain_.index(0, y, z);
   261	                if (domain_.neighbor[X_MINUS] != MPI_PROC_NULL) field[base] = receive_[X_MINUS][p];
   262	                if (domain_.neighbor[X_PLUS] != MPI_PROC_NULL)
   263	                    field[base + domain_.local[0] + 1] = receive_[X_PLUS][p];
   264	                ++p;
   265	            }
   266	        }
   267	    }
   268	
   269	    void unpackY(std::vector<double>& field) {
   270	        size_t p = 0;
   271	        for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain_.local[2]; ++z) {
   272	            const size_t lower = domain_.index(1, 0, z);
   273	            const size_t upper = domain_.index(1, domain_.local[1] + 1, z);
   274	            if (domain_.neighbor[Y_MINUS] != MPI_PROC_NULL)
   275	                std::copy_n(receive_[Y_MINUS].data() + p, domain_.local[0], field.data() + lower);
   276	            if (domain_.neighbor[Y_PLUS] != MPI_PROC_NULL)
   277	                std::copy_n(receive_[Y_PLUS].data() + p, domain_.local[0], field.data() + upper);
   278	            p += domain_.local[0];
   279	        }
   280	    }
   281	
   282	    void unpackZ(std::vector<double>& field) {
   283	        size_t p = 0;
   284	        for (size_t y = 1; y <= domain_.local[1]; ++y) {
   285	            const size_t lower = domain_.index(1, y, 0);
   286	            const size_t upper = domain_.index(1, y, domain_.local[2] + 1);
   287	            if (domain_.neighbor[Z_MINUS] != MPI_PROC_NULL)
   288	                std::copy_n(receive_[Z_MINUS].data() + p, domain_.local[0], field.data() + lower);
   289	            if (domain_.neighbor[Z_PLUS] != MPI_PROC_NULL)
   290	                std::copy_n(receive_[Z_PLUS].data() + p, domain_.local[0], field.data() + upper);
   291	            p += domain_.local[0];
   292	        }
   293	    }
   294	};
   295	
   296	template <class Operation>
   297	inline void computeInterior(const Domain& domain, Operation&& operation) {
   298	    for (size_t z = 2; z < domain.local[2]; ++z)
   299	        for (size_t y = 2; y < domain.local[1]; ++y)
   300	            for (size_t x = 2; x < domain.local[0]; ++x) operation(x, y, z);
   301	}
   302	
   303	template <class Operation>
   304	inline void computeBoundary(const Domain& domain, Operation&& operation) {
   305	    const size_t nx = domain.local[0];
   306	    const size_t ny = domain.local[1];
   307	    const size_t nz = domain.local[2];
   308	
   309	    // X faces include their edges and corners.
   310	    for (size_t z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
   311	        for (size_t y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
   312	            operation(1, y, z);
   313	            if (nx > 1) operation(nx, y, z);
   314	        }
   315	    }
   316	    // Y faces omit the X-face points already handled above.
   317	    for (size_t z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
   318	        for (size_t x = 2; x < nx; ++x) {
   319	            operation(x, 1, z);
   320	            if (ny > 1) operation(x, ny, z);
   321	        }
   322	    }
   323	    // Z faces omit both sets of points already handled above.
   324	    for (size_t y = 2; y < ny; ++y) {
   325	        for (size_t x = 2; x < nx; ++x) {
   326	            operation(x, y, 1);
   327	            if (nz > 1) operation(x, y, nz);
   328	        }
   329	    }
   330	}
   331	
   332	inline double laplacianAt(const std::vector<double>& field, const Domain& domain,
   333	                          const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z,
   334	                          const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) noexcept {
   335	    const size_t i = domain.index(x, y, z);
   336	    const double center = field[i];
   337	    const double cxx = (field[i + 1] + field[i - 1] - 2.0 * center) / (dx * dx);
   338	    const double cyy =
   339	        (field[i + domain.rowStride] + field[i - domain.rowStride] - 2.0 * center) /
   340	        (dy * dy);
   341	    const double czz =
   342	        (field[i + domain.planeStride] + field[i - domain.planeStride] - 2.0 * center) /
   343	        (dz * dz);
   344	    return cxx + cyy + czz;
   345	}
   346	
   347	void computeChemicalPotential(std::vector<double>& concentration, std::vector<double>& mu,
   348	                              HaloExchange& exchange, const Domain& domain, const double dx,
   349	                              const double dy, const double dz, const double gamma,
   350	                              const double eAA, const double eBB, const double eAB) {
   351	    const auto point = [&](const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
   352	        const size_t i = domain.index(x, y, z);
   353	        const double c = concentration[i];
   354	        mu[i] = 4.5 * ((c + 1.0) * eAA + (c - 1.0) * eBB - 2.0 * c * eAB) +
   355	                3.0 * c + c * c * c -
   356	                gamma * laplacianAt(concentration, domain, x, y, z, dx, dy, dz);
   357	    };
   358	
   359	    exchange.begin(concentration);
   360	    computeInterior(domain, point);
   361	    exchange.end(concentration);
   362	    computeBoundary(domain, point);
   363	}
   364	
   365	void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& next, const std::vector<double>& current,
   366	                        std::vector<double>& mu, HaloExchange& exchange, const Domain& domain,
   367	                        const double diffusion, const double dt, const double dx,
   368	                        const double dy, const double dz) {
   369	    const auto point = [&](const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
   370	        const size_t i = domain.index(x, y, z);
   371	        next[i] = current[i] + dt * diffusion * laplacianAt(mu, domain, x, y, z, dx, dy, dz);
   372	    };
   373	
   374	    exchange.begin(mu);
   375	    computeInterior(domain, point);
   376	    exchange.end(mu);
   377	    computeBoundary(domain, point);
   378	}
   379	
   380	void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& concentration, const Domain& domain) {
   381	    const size_t volume = domain.global[0] * domain.global[1] * domain.global[2];
   382	    for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain.local[2]; ++z) {
   383	        const size_t globalZ = domain.offset[2] + z - 1;
   384	        for (size_t y = 1; y <= domain.local[1]; ++y) {
   385	            const size_t globalY = domain.offset[1] + y - 1;
   386	            for (size_t x = 1; x <= domain.local[0]; ++x) {
   387	                const size_t globalX = domain.offset[0] + x - 1;
   388	                const size_t linearId =
   389	                    globalZ * (domain.global[0] * domain.global[1]) +
   390	                    globalY * domain.global[0] + globalX;
   391	                const double pseudo =
   392	                    (((linearId + 1) * 1299709) % volume) / static_cast<double>(volume);
   393	                concentration[domain.index(x, y, z)] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
   394	            }
   395	        }
   396	    }
   397	}
   398	
   399	bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& concentration, const Domain& domain) {
   400	    int localFinite = 1;
   401	    double localMinimum = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
   402	    double localMaximum = -std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
   403	    for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain.local[2]; ++z) {
   404	        for (size_t y = 1; y <= domain.local[1]; ++y) {
   405	            for (size_t x = 1; x <= domain.local[0]; ++x) {
   406	                const double value = concentration[domain.index(x, y, z)];
   407	                if (!std::isfinite(value)) localFinite = 0;
   408	                localMinimum = std::min(localMinimum, value);
   409	                localMaximum = std::max(localMaximum, value);
   410	            }
   411	        }
   412	    }
   413	
   414	    int finite = 0;
   415	    double minimum = 0.0;
   416	    double maximum = 0.0;
   417	    MPI_Allreduce(&localFinite, &finite, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_LAND, domain.comm);
   418	    MPI_Allreduce(&localMinimum, &minimum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, domain.comm);
   419	    MPI_Allreduce(&localMaximum, &maximum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, domain.comm);
   420	
   421	    if (!finite) {
   422	        if (domain.rank == 0) std::printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
   423	        return false;
   424	    }
   425	    if (domain.rank == 0) std::printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minimum, maximum);
   426	    if (maximum > 10.0 || minimum < -10.0) {
   427	        if (domain.rank == 0) std::printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
   428	        return false;
   429	    }
   430	    return true;
   431	}
   432	
   433	std::vector<double> gatherGlobalResult(const std::vector<double>& localField,
   434	                                       const Domain& domain) {
   435	    if (domain.ownedSize() > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
   436	        if (domain.rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "Error: local result exceeds MPI_Gatherv count limit\n");
   437	        MPI_Abort(domain.comm, 2);
   438	    }
   439	    const int localCount = static_cast<int>(domain.ownedSize());
   440	    std::vector<double> packed(domain.ownedSize());
   441	    size_t p = 0;
   442	    for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain.local[2]; ++z)
   443	        for (size_t y = 1; y <= domain.local[1]; ++y) {
   444	            std::copy_n(localField.data() + domain.index(1, y, z), domain.local[0],
   445	                        packed.data() + p);
   446	            p += domain.local[0];
   447	        }
   448	
   449	    std::vector<int> counts;
   450	    std::vector<int> displacements;
   451	    if (domain.rank == 0) {
   452	        counts.resize(domain.processCount);
   453	        displacements.resize(domain.processCount);
   454	    }
   455	    MPI_Gather(&localCount, 1, MPI_INT, counts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, 0, domain.comm);
   456	
   457	    std::vector<double> rankOrdered;
   458	    if (domain.rank == 0) {
   459	        long long total = 0;
   460	        for (int r = 0; r < domain.processCount; ++r) {
   461	            if (total > std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) {
   462	                std::fprintf(stderr, "Error: global result exceeds MPI_Gatherv displacement limit\n");
   463	                MPI_Abort(domain.comm, 2);
   464	            }
   465	            displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(total);
   466	            total += counts[r];
   467	        }
   468	        if (total > std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) {
   469	            std::fprintf(stderr, "Error: global result exceeds MPI_Gatherv count limit\n");
   470	            MPI_Abort(domain.comm, 2);
   471	        }
   472	        rankOrdered.resize(static_cast<size_t>(total));
   473	    }
   474	
   475	    MPI_Gatherv(packed.data(), localCount, MPI_DOUBLE, rankOrdered.data(), counts.data(),
   476	                displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, domain.comm);
   477	
   478	    std::vector<double> globalField;
   479	    if (domain.rank == 0) {
   480	        globalField.resize(domain.global[0] * domain.global[1] * domain.global[2]);
   481	        for (int r = 0; r < domain.processCount; ++r) {
   482	            std::array<int, 3> coordinates{};
   483	            MPI_Cart_coords(domain.comm, r, 3, coordinates.data());
   484	            const Extent ex = splitExtent(domain.global[0], domain.dims[0], coordinates[0]);
   485	            const Extent ey = splitExtent(domain.global[1], domain.dims[1], coordinates[1]);
   486	            const Extent ez = splitExtent(domain.global[2], domain.dims[2], coordinates[2]);
   487	            size_t source = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[r]);
   488	            for (size_t z = 0; z < ez.size; ++z) {
   489	                for (size_t y = 0; y < ey.size; ++y) {
   490	                    const size_t destination =
   491	                        idx3(ex.offset, ey.offset + y, ez.offset + z, domain.global[0],
   492	                             domain.global[1]);
   493	                    std::copy_n(rankOrdered.data() + source, ex.size,
   494	                                globalField.data() + destination);
   495	                    source += ex.size;
   496	                }
   497	            }
   498	        }
   499	    }
   500	    return globalField;
   501	}
   502	
   503	void printUsage(const char* programName) {
   504	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", programName);
   505	    std::printf("Options:\n");
   506	    std::printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
   507	    std::printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
   508	    std::printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
   509	    std::printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
   510	    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   511	    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   512	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   513	}
   514	
   515	}  // namespace
   516	
   517	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   518	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   519	    int worldRank = 0;
   520	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank);
   520	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank);
   521	
   522	    size_t nx = 64;
   523	    size_t ny = 0;
   524	    size_t nz = 0;
   525	    int iterations = 20;
   526	    bool validate = false;
   527	    bool printResults = false;
   528	    bool help = false;
   529	    bool argumentsValid = true;
   530	
   531	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   532	        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   533	            nx = static_cast<size_t>(std::strtoull(argv[++i], nullptr, 10));
   534	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   535	            ny = static_cast<size_t>(std::strtoull(argv[++i], nullptr, 10));
   536	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   537	            nz = static_cast<size_t>(std::strtoull(argv[++i], nullptr, 10));
   538	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   539	            iterations = std::atoi(argv[++i]);
   540	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   541	            validate = true;
   542	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   543	            printResults = true;
   544	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   545	            help = true;
   546	        } else {
   547	            if (worldRank == 0) std::printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   548	            argumentsValid = false;
   549	            break;
   550	        }
   551	    }
   552	
   553	    if (help || !argumentsValid) {
   554	        if (worldRank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);
   555	        MPI_Finalize();
   556	        return argumentsValid ? 0 : 1;
   557	    }
   558	    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
   559	    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
   560	    if (nx == 0 || ny == 0 || nz == 0 || iterations < 0 ||
   561	        nx > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / ny ||
   562	        nx * ny > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / nz) {
   563	        if (worldRank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "Error: grid dimensions and iterations must be valid\n");
   564	        MPI_Finalize();
   565	        return 1;
   566	    }
   567	
   568	    int returnCode = 0;
   569	    {
   570	        Domain domain(nx, ny, nz);
   571	        if (domain.rank == 0) {
   572	            std::printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
   573	            std::printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
   574	            std::printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
   575	            std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   576	            std::printf("MPI processes: %d (%d x %d x %d process grid)\n", domain.processCount,
   577	                        domain.dims[0], domain.dims[1], domain.dims[2]);
   578	        }
   579	
   580	        const double dx = 1.0;
   581	        const double dy = 1.0;
   582	        const double dz = 1.0;
   583	        const double dt = 0.01;
   584	        const double eAA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
   585	        const double eBB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
   586	        const double eAB = (2.0 / 9.0);
   587	        const double gamma = 0.5;
   588	        const double diffusion = 1.0;
   589	        const size_t globalSize = nx * ny * nz;
   590	
   591	        std::vector<double> current(domain.allocatedSize());
   592	        std::vector<double> next(domain.allocatedSize());
   593	        std::vector<double> mu(domain.allocatedSize());
   594	
   595	        if (domain.rank == 0) std::printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
   596	        initializeConcentration(current, domain);
   597	        if (domain.rank == 0) std::printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
   598	
   599	        double localSeconds = 0.0;
   600	        {
   601	            HaloExchange exchange(domain);
   602	            MPI_Barrier(domain.comm);
   603	            const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   604	            for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
   605	                computeChemicalPotential(current, mu, exchange, domain, dx, dy, dz, gamma, eAA,
   606	                                         eBB, eAB);
   607	                cahnHilliardUpdate(next, current, mu, exchange, domain, diffusion, dt, dx, dy, dz);
   608	                std::swap(current, next);
   609	            }
   610	            localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   611	        }
   612	
   613	        double seconds = 0.0;
   614	        MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &seconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, domain.comm);
   615	        if (domain.rank == 0) {
   616	            const long milliseconds = static_cast<long>(seconds * 1000.0);
   617	            const double cellUpdates = static_cast<double>(globalSize) * iterations;
   618	            const double mcups = seconds > 0.0 ? cellUpdates / seconds / 1.0e6 : 0.0;
   619	            std::printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", milliseconds);
   620	            std::printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
   621	        }
   622	
   623	        if (printResults) {
   624	            std::vector<double> globalResult = gatherGlobalResult(current, domain);
   625	            if (domain.rank == 0) print_results(globalResult, "Concentration");
   626	        }
   627	
   628	        if (validate) {
   629	            if (domain.rank == 0) std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   630	            const bool valid = validateResult(current, domain);
   631	            if (domain.rank == 0) std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
   632	            if (!valid) returnCode = 1;
   633	        }
   634	    }
   635	
   636	    MPI_Finalize();
   637	    return returnCode;
   638	}

+struct Extent {
+    size_t size;
+    size_t offset;
+};
+
+Extent splitExtent(const size_t globalSize, const int partitions, const int coordinate) {
+    const size_t count = globalSize / static_cast<size_t>(partitions);
+    const size_t remainder = globalSize % static_cast<size_t>(partitions);
+    const size_t extra = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) < remainder ? 1 : 0;
+    return {count + extra,
+            static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) * count +
+                std::min(static_cast<size_t>(coordinate), remainder)};

-// Compute chemical potential
-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,

-                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {

+// Choose a process grid that minimizes the slowest rank's work and then the
+// total inter-rank interface area.  The final tie-break favors Z/Y cuts because
+// their faces are cheaper to pack from the X-major storage layout.
+std::array<int, 3> chooseProcessGrid(const int processCount, const size_t nx,
+                                     const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
+    std::array<int, 3> best = {0, 0, 0};
+    long double bestScore = std::numeric_limits<long double>::infinity();
+
+    for (int px = 1; px <= processCount && static_cast<size_t>(px) <= nx; ++px) {
+        if (processCount % px != 0) continue;
+        const int yz = processCount / px;
+        for (int py = 1; py <= yz && static_cast<size_t>(py) <= ny; ++py) {
+            if (yz % py != 0) continue;
+            const int pz = yz / py;
+            if (static_cast<size_t>(pz) > nz) continue;
+
+            const long double maxVolume =
+                static_cast<long double>((nx + px - 1) / px) *
+                static_cast<long double>((ny + py - 1) / py) *
+                static_cast<long double>((nz + pz - 1) / pz);
+            const long double interfaces =
+                static_cast<long double>(px - 1) * ny * nz +
+                static_cast<long double>(py - 1) * nx * nz +
+                static_cast<long double>(pz - 1) * nx * ny;
+            const long double score = maxVolume + interfaces;
+
+            const bool betterTie = score == bestScore &&
+                (pz > best[2] || (pz == best[2] && py > best[1]));
+            if (score < bestScore || betterTie) {
+                bestScore = score;
+                best = {px, py, pz};
             }
         }
     }
+    return best;
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+struct Domain {
+    MPI_Comm comm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    int rank = 0;
+    int processCount = 1;
+    std::array<int, 3> dims{};
+    std::array<int, 3> coords{};
+    std::array<int, 6> neighbor{};
+    std::array<size_t, 3> global{};
+    std::array<size_t, 3> local{};
+    std::array<size_t, 3> offset{};
+    size_t rowStride = 0;
+    size_t planeStride = 0;
+
+    Domain(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) : global{nx, ny, nz} {
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &processCount);
+        dims = chooseProcessGrid(processCount, nx, ny, nz);
+        if (dims[0] == 0) {
+            if (processCount > 0) {
+                int worldRank = 0;
+                MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank);
+                if (worldRank == 0) {
+                    std::fprintf(stderr,
+                                 "Error: the %zu x %zu x %zu grid cannot be split among %d nonempty MPI ranks\n",
+                                 nx, ny, nz, processCount);
+                }
+            }
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+        }
+
+        const int periods[3] = {0, 0, 0};
+        MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 3, dims.data(), periods, 0, &comm);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
+        MPI_Cart_coords(comm, rank, 3, coords.data());
+        MPI_Cart_shift(comm, 0, 1, &neighbor[X_MINUS], &neighbor[X_PLUS]);
+        MPI_Cart_shift(comm, 1, 1, &neighbor[Y_MINUS], &neighbor[Y_PLUS]);
+        MPI_Cart_shift(comm, 2, 1, &neighbor[Z_MINUS], &neighbor[Z_PLUS]);
+
+        for (int d = 0; d < 3; ++d) {
+            const Extent extent = splitExtent(global[d], dims[d], coords[d]);
+            local[d] = extent.size;
+            offset[d] = extent.offset;
+        }
+        rowStride = local[0] + 2;
+        planeStride = rowStride * (local[1] + 2);
+    }
+
+    ~Domain() {
+        if (comm != MPI_COMM_NULL) MPI_Comm_free(&comm);
+    }
+
+    Domain(const Domain&) = delete;
+    Domain& operator=(const Domain&) = delete;
+
+    size_t index(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) const noexcept {
+        return z * planeStride + y * rowStride + x;
+    }
+
+    size_t allocatedSize() const noexcept { return planeStride * (local[2] + 2); }
+    size_t ownedSize() const noexcept { return local[0] * local[1] * local[2]; }
+};
+
+class HaloExchange {
+  public:
+    explicit HaloExchange(const Domain& domain) : domain_(domain) {
+        const size_t xFace = domain_.local[1] * domain_.local[2];
+        const size_t yFace = domain_.local[0] * domain_.local[2];
+        const size_t zFace = domain_.local[0] * domain_.local[1];
+        const std::array<size_t, 6> sizes = {xFace, xFace, yFace, yFace, zFace, zFace};
+
+        for (int face = 0; face < 6; ++face) {
+            if (sizes[face] > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+                if (domain_.rank == 0) {
+                    std::fprintf(stderr, "Error: an MPI halo exceeds the implementation's count limit\n");
+                }
+                MPI_Abort(domain_.comm, 2);
+            }
+            send_[face].resize(sizes[face]);
+            receive_[face].resize(sizes[face]);
+            if (domain_.neighbor[face] == MPI_PROC_NULL) continue;
+
+            MPI_Request receiveRequest;
+            MPI_Request sendRequest;
+            MPI_Recv_init(receive_[face].data(), static_cast<int>(sizes[face]), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                          domain_.neighbor[face], 100 + oppositeFace(face), domain_.comm,
+                          &receiveRequest);
+            MPI_Send_init(send_[face].data(), static_cast<int>(sizes[face]), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                          domain_.neighbor[face], 100 + face, domain_.comm, &sendRequest);
+            requests_.push_back(receiveRequest);
+            requests_.push_back(sendRequest);
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~HaloExchange() {
+        for (MPI_Request& request : requests_) MPI_Request_free(&request);
+    }
+
+    HaloExchange(const HaloExchange&) = delete;
+    HaloExchange& operator=(const HaloExchange&) = delete;
+
+    void begin(std::vector<double>& field) {
+        packOrClampX(field);
+        packOrClampY(field);
+        packOrClampZ(field);
+        if (!requests_.empty()) {
+            MPI_Startall(static_cast<int>(requests_.size()), requests_.data());
+        }
+    }
+
+    void end(std::vector<double>& field) {
+        if (!requests_.empty()) {
+            MPI_Waitall(static_cast<int>(requests_.size()), requests_.data(), MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        }
+        unpackX(field);
+        unpackY(field);
+        unpackZ(field);
+    }
+
+  private:
+    const Domain& domain_;
+    std::array<std::vector<double>, 6> send_;
+    std::array<std::vector<double>, 6> receive_;
+    std::vector<MPI_Request> requests_;
+
+    void packOrClampX(std::vector<double>& field) {
+        size_t p = 0;
+        for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain_.local[2]; ++z) {
+            for (size_t y = 1; y <= domain_.local[1]; ++y) {
+                const size_t base = domain_.index(0, y, z);
+                if (domain_.neighbor[X_MINUS] == MPI_PROC_NULL) field[base] = field[base + 1];
+                else send_[X_MINUS][p] = field[base + 1];
+                if (domain_.neighbor[X_PLUS] == MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                    field[base + domain_.local[0] + 1] = field[base + domain_.local[0]];
+                else
+                    send_[X_PLUS][p] = field[base + domain_.local[0]];
+                ++p;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    void packOrClampY(std::vector<double>& field) {
+        size_t p = 0;
+        for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain_.local[2]; ++z) {
+            const size_t lower = domain_.index(1, 0, z);
+            const size_t upper = domain_.index(1, domain_.local[1], z);
+            if (domain_.neighbor[Y_MINUS] == MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                std::copy_n(field.data() + lower + domain_.rowStride, domain_.local[0],
+                            field.data() + lower);
+            else {
+                std::copy_n(field.data() + lower + domain_.rowStride, domain_.local[0],
+                            send_[Y_MINUS].data() + p);
+            }
+            if (domain_.neighbor[Y_PLUS] == MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                std::copy_n(field.data() + upper, domain_.local[0],
+                            field.data() + upper + domain_.rowStride);
+            else
+                std::copy_n(field.data() + upper, domain_.local[0], send_[Y_PLUS].data() + p);
+            p += domain_.local[0];
+        }
+    }
+
+    void packOrClampZ(std::vector<double>& field) {
+        size_t p = 0;
+        for (size_t y = 1; y <= domain_.local[1]; ++y) {
+            const size_t lower = domain_.index(1, y, 0);
+            const size_t upper = domain_.index(1, y, domain_.local[2]);
+            if (domain_.neighbor[Z_MINUS] == MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                std::copy_n(field.data() + lower + domain_.planeStride, domain_.local[0],
+                            field.data() + lower);
+            else
+                std::copy_n(field.data() + lower + domain_.planeStride, domain_.local[0],
+                            send_[Z_MINUS].data() + p);
+            if (domain_.neighbor[Z_PLUS] == MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                std::copy_n(field.data() + upper, domain_.local[0],
+                            field.data() + upper + domain_.planeStride);
+            else
+                std::copy_n(field.data() + upper, domain_.local[0], send_[Z_PLUS].data() + p);
+            p += domain_.local[0];
+        }
+    }
+
+    void unpackX(std::vector<double>& field) {
+        size_t p = 0;
+        for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain_.local[2]; ++z) {
+            for (size_t y = 1; y <= domain_.local[1]; ++y) {
+                const size_t base = domain_.index(0, y, z);
+                if (domain_.neighbor[X_MINUS] != MPI_PROC_NULL) field[base] = receive_[X_MINUS][p];
+                if (domain_.neighbor[X_PLUS] != MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                    field[base + domain_.local[0] + 1] = receive_[X_PLUS][p];
+                ++p;
             }
         }
     }
+
+    void unpackY(std::vector<double>& field) {
+        size_t p = 0;
+        for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain_.local[2]; ++z) {
+            const size_t lower = domain_.index(1, 0, z);
+            const size_t upper = domain_.index(1, domain_.local[1] + 1, z);
+            if (domain_.neighbor[Y_MINUS] != MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                std::copy_n(receive_[Y_MINUS].data() + p, domain_.local[0], field.data() + lower);
+            if (domain_.neighbor[Y_PLUS] != MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                std::copy_n(receive_[Y_PLUS].data() + p, domain_.local[0], field.data() + upper);
+            p += domain_.local[0];
+        }
+    }
+
+    void unpackZ(std::vector<double>& field) {
+        size_t p = 0;
+        for (size_t y = 1; y <= domain_.local[1]; ++y) {
+            const size_t lower = domain_.index(1, y, 0);
+            const size_t upper = domain_.index(1, y, domain_.local[2] + 1);
+            if (domain_.neighbor[Z_MINUS] != MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                std::copy_n(receive_[Z_MINUS].data() + p, domain_.local[0], field.data() + lower);
+            if (domain_.neighbor[Z_PLUS] != MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                std::copy_n(receive_[Z_PLUS].data() + p, domain_.local[0], field.data() + upper);
+            p += domain_.local[0];
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+template <class Operation>
+inline void computeInterior(const Domain& domain, Operation&& operation) {
+    for (size_t z = 2; z < domain.local[2]; ++z)
+        for (size_t y = 2; y < domain.local[1]; ++y)
+            for (size_t x = 2; x < domain.local[0]; ++x) operation(x, y, z);
 }
 
-// Initialize concentration field
-void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
-    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
-    
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]
-                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
-                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
-                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+template <class Operation>
+inline void computeBoundary(const Domain& domain, Operation&& operation) {
+    const size_t nx = domain.local[0];
+    const size_t ny = domain.local[1];
+    const size_t nz = domain.local[2];
+
+    // X faces include their edges and corners.
+    for (size_t z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            operation(1, y, z);
+            if (nx > 1) operation(nx, y, z);
+        }
+    }
+    // Y faces omit the X-face points already handled above.
+    for (size_t z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (size_t x = 2; x < nx; ++x) {
+            operation(x, 1, z);
+            if (ny > 1) operation(x, ny, z);
+        }
+    }
+    // Z faces omit both sets of points already handled above.
+    for (size_t y = 2; y < ny; ++y) {
+        for (size_t x = 2; x < nx; ++x) {
+            operation(x, y, 1);
+            if (nz > 1) operation(x, y, nz);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+inline double laplacianAt(const std::vector<double>& field, const Domain& domain,
+                          const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z,
+                          const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) noexcept {
+    const size_t i = domain.index(x, y, z);
+    const double center = field[i];
+    const double cxx = (field[i + 1] + field[i - 1] - 2.0 * center) / (dx * dx);
+    const double cyy =
+        (field[i + domain.rowStride] + field[i - domain.rowStride] - 2.0 * center) /
+        (dy * dy);
+    const double czz =
+        (field[i + domain.planeStride] + field[i - domain.planeStride] - 2.0 * center) /
+        (dz * dz);
+    return cxx + cyy + czz;
+}
+
+void computeChemicalPotential(std::vector<double>& concentration, std::vector<double>& mu,
+                              HaloExchange& exchange, const Domain& domain, const double dx,
+                              const double dy, const double dz, const double gamma,
+                              const double eAA, const double eBB, const double eAB) {
+    const auto point = [&](const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
+        const size_t i = domain.index(x, y, z);
+        const double c = concentration[i];
+        mu[i] = 4.5 * ((c + 1.0) * eAA + (c - 1.0) * eBB - 2.0 * c * eAB) +
+                3.0 * c + c * c * c -
+                gamma * laplacianAt(concentration, domain, x, y, z, dx, dy, dz);
+    };
+
+    exchange.begin(concentration);
+    computeInterior(domain, point);
+    exchange.end(concentration);
+    computeBoundary(domain, point);
+}
+
+void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& next, const std::vector<double>& current,
+                        std::vector<double>& mu, HaloExchange& exchange, const Domain& domain,
+                        const double diffusion, const double dt, const double dx,
+                        const double dy, const double dz) {
+    const auto point = [&](const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
+        const size_t i = domain.index(x, y, z);
+        next[i] = current[i] + dt * diffusion * laplacianAt(mu, domain, x, y, z, dx, dy, dz);
+    };
+
+    exchange.begin(mu);
+    computeInterior(domain, point);
+    exchange.end(mu);
+    computeBoundary(domain, point);
+}
+
+void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& concentration, const Domain& domain) {
+    const size_t volume = domain.global[0] * domain.global[1] * domain.global[2];
+    for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain.local[2]; ++z) {
+        const size_t globalZ = domain.offset[2] + z - 1;
+        for (size_t y = 1; y <= domain.local[1]; ++y) {
+            const size_t globalY = domain.offset[1] + y - 1;
+            for (size_t x = 1; x <= domain.local[0]; ++x) {
+                const size_t globalX = domain.offset[0] + x - 1;
+                const size_t linearId =
+                    globalZ * (domain.global[0] * domain.global[1]) +
+                    globalY * domain.global[0] + globalX;
+                const double pseudo =
+                    (((linearId + 1) * 1299709) % volume) / static_cast<double>(volume);
+                concentration[domain.index(x, y, z)] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
             }
         }
     }
 }

+std::vector<double> gatherGlobalResult(const std::vector<double>& localField,
+                                       const Domain& domain) {
+    if (domain.ownedSize() > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        if (domain.rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "Error: local result exceeds MPI_Gatherv count limit\n");
+        MPI_Abort(domain.comm, 2);
+    }
+    const int localCount = static_cast<int>(domain.ownedSize());
+    std::vector<double> packed(domain.ownedSize());
+    size_t p = 0;
+    for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain.local[2]; ++z)
+        for (size_t y = 1; y <= domain.local[1]; ++y) {
+            std::copy_n(localField.data() + domain.index(1, y, z), domain.local[0],
+                        packed.data() + p);
+            p += domain.local[0];
+        }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (domain.rank == 0) {
+        counts.resize(domain.processCount);
+        displacements.resize(domain.processCount);
+    }
+    MPI_Gather(&localCount, 1, MPI_INT, counts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, 0, domain.comm);
+
+    std::vector<double> rankOrdered;
+    if (domain.rank == 0) {
+        long long total = 0;
+        for (int r = 0; r < domain.processCount; ++r) {
+            if (total > std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) {
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Error: global result exceeds MPI_Gatherv displacement limit\n");
+                MPI_Abort(domain.comm, 2);
+            }
+            displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(total);
+            total += counts[r];
+        }
+        if (total > std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Error: global result exceeds MPI_Gatherv count limit\n");
+            MPI_Abort(domain.comm, 2);
+        }
+        rankOrdered.resize(static_cast<size_t>(total));
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(packed.data(), localCount, MPI_DOUBLE, rankOrdered.data(), counts.data(),
+                displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, domain.comm);
+
+    std::vector<double> globalField;
+    if (domain.rank == 0) {
+        globalField.resize(domain.global[0] * domain.global[1] * domain.global[2]);
+        for (int r = 0; r < domain.processCount; ++r) {
+            std::array<int, 3> coordinates{};
+            MPI_Cart_coords(domain.comm, r, 3, coordinates.data());
+            const Extent ex = splitExtent(domain.global[0], domain.dims[0], coordinates[0]);
+            const Extent ey = splitExtent(domain.global[1], domain.dims[1], coordinates[1]);
+            const Extent ez = splitExtent(domain.global[2], domain.dims[2], coordinates[2]);
+            size_t source = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[r]);
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < ez.size; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < ey.size; ++y) {
+                    const size_t destination =
+                        idx3(ex.offset, ey.offset + y, ez.offset + z, domain.global[0],
+                             domain.global[1]);
+                    std::copy_n(rankOrdered.data() + source, ex.size,
+                                globalField.data() + destination);
+                    source += ex.size;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return globalField;
+}

codex
Implemented unconditional MPI distributed-memory parallelism while preserving the `cahn_hilliard` executable name.

Key changes:

- 3D Cartesian domain decomposition with load-aware process-grid selection.
- Persistent nonblocking halo exchanges in all six directions.
- Communication/computation overlap using separate interior and boundary kernels.
- Distributed initialization and validation.
- Global result reconstruction on rank 0 only for `-r`.
- MPI-aware timing using the slowest rank.
- Required MPI integration in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/cahn-hilliard_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r4/cahn-hilliard/CMakeLists.txt:8).
- Main implementation in [cahn_hilliard.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/cahn-hilliard_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r4/cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp:39).

Validation completed:

- Bitwise-identical output to the original across 1–12 ranks and uneven grids.
- Default result hash: `945edb7faf25aaee`.
- AddressSanitizer and UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer passed.

+struct Extent {
+    size_t size;
+    size_t offset;

+Extent splitExtent(const size_t globalSize, const int partitions, const int coordinate) {
+    const size_t count = globalSize / static_cast<size_t>(partitions);
+    const size_t remainder = globalSize % static_cast<size_t>(partitions);
+    const size_t extra = static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) < remainder ? 1 : 0;
+    return {count + extra,
+            static_cast<size_t>(coordinate) * count +
+                std::min(static_cast<size_t>(coordinate), remainder)};

-void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,

-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {

+// Choose a process grid that minimizes the slowest rank's work and then the
+// total inter-rank interface area.  The final tie-break favors Z/Y cuts because
+// their faces are cheaper to pack from the X-major storage layout.
+std::array<int, 3> chooseProcessGrid(const int processCount, const size_t nx,
+                                     const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
+    std::array<int, 3> best = {0, 0, 0};
+    long double bestScore = std::numeric_limits<long double>::infinity();
+
+    for (int px = 1; px <= processCount && static_cast<size_t>(px) <= nx; ++px) {
+        if (processCount % px != 0) continue;
+        const int yz = processCount / px;
+        for (int py = 1; py <= yz && static_cast<size_t>(py) <= ny; ++py) {
+            if (yz % py != 0) continue;
+            const int pz = yz / py;
+            if (static_cast<size_t>(pz) > nz) continue;
+
+            const long double maxVolume =
+                static_cast<long double>((nx + px - 1) / px) *
+                static_cast<long double>((ny + py - 1) / py) *
+                static_cast<long double>((nz + pz - 1) / pz);
+            const long double interfaces =
+                static_cast<long double>(px - 1) * ny * nz +
+                static_cast<long double>(py - 1) * nx * nz +
+                static_cast<long double>(pz - 1) * nx * ny;
+            const long double score = maxVolume + interfaces;
+
+            const bool betterTie = score == bestScore &&
+                (pz > best[2] || (pz == best[2] && py > best[1]));
+            if (score < bestScore || betterTie) {
+                bestScore = score;
+                best = {px, py, pz};
             }
         }
     }
+    return best;
 }
 
-// Cahn-Hilliard update step
-void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
-                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
-                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
-                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
-                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
+struct Domain {
+    MPI_Comm comm = MPI_COMM_NULL;
+    int rank = 0;
+    int processCount = 1;
+    std::array<int, 3> dims{};
+    std::array<int, 3> coords{};
+    std::array<int, 6> neighbor{};
+    std::array<size_t, 3> global{};
+    std::array<size_t, 3> local{};
+    std::array<size_t, 3> offset{};
+    size_t rowStride = 0;
+    size_t planeStride = 0;
+
+    Domain(const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) : global{nx, ny, nz} {
+        MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &processCount);
+        dims = chooseProcessGrid(processCount, nx, ny, nz);
+        if (dims[0] == 0) {
+            if (processCount > 0) {
+                int worldRank = 0;
+                MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldRank);
+                if (worldRank == 0) {
+                    std::fprintf(stderr,
+                                 "Error: the %zu x %zu x %zu grid cannot be split among %d nonempty MPI ranks\n",
+                                 nx, ny, nz, processCount);
+                }
+            }
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+        }
+
+        const int periods[3] = {0, 0, 0};
+        MPI_Cart_create(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 3, dims.data(), periods, 0, &comm);
+        MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
+        MPI_Cart_coords(comm, rank, 3, coords.data());
+        MPI_Cart_shift(comm, 0, 1, &neighbor[X_MINUS], &neighbor[X_PLUS]);
+        MPI_Cart_shift(comm, 1, 1, &neighbor[Y_MINUS], &neighbor[Y_PLUS]);
+        MPI_Cart_shift(comm, 2, 1, &neighbor[Z_MINUS], &neighbor[Z_PLUS]);
+
+        for (int d = 0; d < 3; ++d) {
+            const Extent extent = splitExtent(global[d], dims[d], coords[d]);
+            local[d] = extent.size;
+            offset[d] = extent.offset;
+        }
+        rowStride = local[0] + 2;
+        planeStride = rowStride * (local[1] + 2);
+    }
+
+    ~Domain() {
+        if (comm != MPI_COMM_NULL) MPI_Comm_free(&comm);
+    }
+
+    Domain(const Domain&) = delete;
+    Domain& operator=(const Domain&) = delete;
+
+    size_t index(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) const noexcept {
+        return z * planeStride + y * rowStride + x;
+    }
+
+    size_t allocatedSize() const noexcept { return planeStride * (local[2] + 2); }
+    size_t ownedSize() const noexcept { return local[0] * local[1] * local[2]; }
+};
+
+class HaloExchange {
+  public:
+    explicit HaloExchange(const Domain& domain) : domain_(domain) {
+        const size_t xFace = domain_.local[1] * domain_.local[2];
+        const size_t yFace = domain_.local[0] * domain_.local[2];
+        const size_t zFace = domain_.local[0] * domain_.local[1];
+        const std::array<size_t, 6> sizes = {xFace, xFace, yFace, yFace, zFace, zFace};
+
+        for (int face = 0; face < 6; ++face) {
+            if (sizes[face] > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+                if (domain_.rank == 0) {
+                    std::fprintf(stderr, "Error: an MPI halo exceeds the implementation's count limit\n");
+                }
+                MPI_Abort(domain_.comm, 2);
+            }
+            send_[face].resize(sizes[face]);
+            receive_[face].resize(sizes[face]);
+            if (domain_.neighbor[face] == MPI_PROC_NULL) continue;
+
+            MPI_Request receiveRequest;
+            MPI_Request sendRequest;
+            MPI_Recv_init(receive_[face].data(), static_cast<int>(sizes[face]), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                          domain_.neighbor[face], 100 + oppositeFace(face), domain_.comm,
+                          &receiveRequest);
+            MPI_Send_init(send_[face].data(), static_cast<int>(sizes[face]), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                          domain_.neighbor[face], 100 + face, domain_.comm, &sendRequest);
+            requests_.push_back(receiveRequest);
+            requests_.push_back(sendRequest);
+        }
+    }
+
+    ~HaloExchange() {
+        for (MPI_Request& request : requests_) MPI_Request_free(&request);
+    }
+
+    HaloExchange(const HaloExchange&) = delete;
+    HaloExchange& operator=(const HaloExchange&) = delete;
+
+    void begin(std::vector<double>& field) {
+        packOrClampX(field);
+        packOrClampY(field);
+        packOrClampZ(field);
+        if (!requests_.empty()) {
+            MPI_Startall(static_cast<int>(requests_.size()), requests_.data());
+        }
+    }
+
+    void end(std::vector<double>& field) {
+        if (!requests_.empty()) {
+            MPI_Waitall(static_cast<int>(requests_.size()), requests_.data(), MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        }
+        unpackX(field);
+        unpackY(field);
+        unpackZ(field);
+    }
+
+  private:
+    const Domain& domain_;
+    std::array<std::vector<double>, 6> send_;
+    std::array<std::vector<double>, 6> receive_;
+    std::vector<MPI_Request> requests_;
+
+    void packOrClampX(std::vector<double>& field) {
+        size_t p = 0;
+        for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain_.local[2]; ++z) {
+            for (size_t y = 1; y <= domain_.local[1]; ++y) {
+                const size_t base = domain_.index(0, y, z);
+                if (domain_.neighbor[X_MINUS] == MPI_PROC_NULL) field[base] = field[base + 1];
+                else send_[X_MINUS][p] = field[base + 1];
+                if (domain_.neighbor[X_PLUS] == MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                    field[base + domain_.local[0] + 1] = field[base + domain_.local[0]];
+                else
+                    send_[X_PLUS][p] = field[base + domain_.local[0]];
+                ++p;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    void packOrClampY(std::vector<double>& field) {
+        size_t p = 0;
+        for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain_.local[2]; ++z) {
+            const size_t lower = domain_.index(1, 0, z);
+            const size_t upper = domain_.index(1, domain_.local[1], z);
+            if (domain_.neighbor[Y_MINUS] == MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                std::copy_n(field.data() + lower + domain_.rowStride, domain_.local[0],
+                            field.data() + lower);
+            else {
+                std::copy_n(field.data() + lower + domain_.rowStride, domain_.local[0],
+                            send_[Y_MINUS].data() + p);
+            }
+            if (domain_.neighbor[Y_PLUS] == MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                std::copy_n(field.data() + upper, domain_.local[0],
+                            field.data() + upper + domain_.rowStride);
+            else
+                std::copy_n(field.data() + upper, domain_.local[0], send_[Y_PLUS].data() + p);
+            p += domain_.local[0];
+        }
+    }
+
+    void packOrClampZ(std::vector<double>& field) {
+        size_t p = 0;
+        for (size_t y = 1; y <= domain_.local[1]; ++y) {
+            const size_t lower = domain_.index(1, y, 0);
+            const size_t upper = domain_.index(1, y, domain_.local[2]);
+            if (domain_.neighbor[Z_MINUS] == MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                std::copy_n(field.data() + lower + domain_.planeStride, domain_.local[0],
+                            field.data() + lower);
+            else
+                std::copy_n(field.data() + lower + domain_.planeStride, domain_.local[0],
+                            send_[Z_MINUS].data() + p);
+            if (domain_.neighbor[Z_PLUS] == MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                std::copy_n(field.data() + upper, domain_.local[0],
+                            field.data() + upper + domain_.planeStride);
+            else
+                std::copy_n(field.data() + upper, domain_.local[0], send_[Z_PLUS].data() + p);
+            p += domain_.local[0];
+        }
+    }
+
+    void unpackX(std::vector<double>& field) {
+        size_t p = 0;
+        for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain_.local[2]; ++z) {
+            for (size_t y = 1; y <= domain_.local[1]; ++y) {
+                const size_t base = domain_.index(0, y, z);
+                if (domain_.neighbor[X_MINUS] != MPI_PROC_NULL) field[base] = receive_[X_MINUS][p];
+                if (domain_.neighbor[X_PLUS] != MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                    field[base + domain_.local[0] + 1] = receive_[X_PLUS][p];
+                ++p;
             }
         }
     }
+
+    void unpackY(std::vector<double>& field) {
+        size_t p = 0;
+        for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain_.local[2]; ++z) {
+            const size_t lower = domain_.index(1, 0, z);
+            const size_t upper = domain_.index(1, domain_.local[1] + 1, z);
+            if (domain_.neighbor[Y_MINUS] != MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                std::copy_n(receive_[Y_MINUS].data() + p, domain_.local[0], field.data() + lower);
+            if (domain_.neighbor[Y_PLUS] != MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                std::copy_n(receive_[Y_PLUS].data() + p, domain_.local[0], field.data() + upper);
+            p += domain_.local[0];
+        }
+    }
+
+    void unpackZ(std::vector<double>& field) {
+        size_t p = 0;
+        for (size_t y = 1; y <= domain_.local[1]; ++y) {
+            const size_t lower = domain_.index(1, y, 0);
+            const size_t upper = domain_.index(1, y, domain_.local[2] + 1);
+            if (domain_.neighbor[Z_MINUS] != MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                std::copy_n(receive_[Z_MINUS].data() + p, domain_.local[0], field.data() + lower);
+            if (domain_.neighbor[Z_PLUS] != MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                std::copy_n(receive_[Z_PLUS].data() + p, domain_.local[0], field.data() + upper);
+            p += domain_.local[0];
+        }
+    }
+};
+
+template <class Operation>
+inline void computeInterior(const Domain& domain, Operation&& operation) {
+    for (size_t z = 2; z < domain.local[2]; ++z)
+        for (size_t y = 2; y < domain.local[1]; ++y)
+            for (size_t x = 2; x < domain.local[0]; ++x) operation(x, y, z);
 }
 
-// Initialize concentration field
-void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
-    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
-    
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]
-                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
-                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
-                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
+template <class Operation>
+inline void computeBoundary(const Domain& domain, Operation&& operation) {
+    const size_t nx = domain.local[0];
+    const size_t ny = domain.local[1];
+    const size_t nz = domain.local[2];
+
+    // X faces include their edges and corners.
+    for (size_t z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (size_t y = 1; y <= ny; ++y) {
+            operation(1, y, z);
+            if (nx > 1) operation(nx, y, z);
+        }
+    }
+    // Y faces omit the X-face points already handled above.
+    for (size_t z = 1; z <= nz; ++z) {
+        for (size_t x = 2; x < nx; ++x) {
+            operation(x, 1, z);
+            if (ny > 1) operation(x, ny, z);
+        }
+    }
+    // Z faces omit both sets of points already handled above.
+    for (size_t y = 2; y < ny; ++y) {
+        for (size_t x = 2; x < nx; ++x) {
+            operation(x, y, 1);
+            if (nz > 1) operation(x, y, nz);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+inline double laplacianAt(const std::vector<double>& field, const Domain& domain,
+                          const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z,
+                          const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) noexcept {
+    const size_t i = domain.index(x, y, z);
+    const double center = field[i];
+    const double cxx = (field[i + 1] + field[i - 1] - 2.0 * center) / (dx * dx);
+    const double cyy =
+        (field[i + domain.rowStride] + field[i - domain.rowStride] - 2.0 * center) /
+        (dy * dy);
+    const double czz =
+        (field[i + domain.planeStride] + field[i - domain.planeStride] - 2.0 * center) /
+        (dz * dz);
+    return cxx + cyy + czz;
+}
+
+void computeChemicalPotential(std::vector<double>& concentration, std::vector<double>& mu,
+                              HaloExchange& exchange, const Domain& domain, const double dx,
+                              const double dy, const double dz, const double gamma,
+                              const double eAA, const double eBB, const double eAB) {
+    const auto point = [&](const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
+        const size_t i = domain.index(x, y, z);
+        const double c = concentration[i];
+        mu[i] = 4.5 * ((c + 1.0) * eAA + (c - 1.0) * eBB - 2.0 * c * eAB) +
+                3.0 * c + c * c * c -
+                gamma * laplacianAt(concentration, domain, x, y, z, dx, dy, dz);
+    };
+
+    exchange.begin(concentration);
+    computeInterior(domain, point);
+    exchange.end(concentration);
+    computeBoundary(domain, point);
+}
+
+void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& next, const std::vector<double>& current,
+                        std::vector<double>& mu, HaloExchange& exchange, const Domain& domain,
+                        const double diffusion, const double dt, const double dx,
+                        const double dy, const double dz) {
+    const auto point = [&](const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
+        const size_t i = domain.index(x, y, z);
+        next[i] = current[i] + dt * diffusion * laplacianAt(mu, domain, x, y, z, dx, dy, dz);
+    };
+
+    exchange.begin(mu);
+    computeInterior(domain, point);
+    exchange.end(mu);
+    computeBoundary(domain, point);
+}
+
+void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& concentration, const Domain& domain) {
+    const size_t volume = domain.global[0] * domain.global[1] * domain.global[2];
+    for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain.local[2]; ++z) {
+        const size_t globalZ = domain.offset[2] + z - 1;
+        for (size_t y = 1; y <= domain.local[1]; ++y) {
+            const size_t globalY = domain.offset[1] + y - 1;
+            for (size_t x = 1; x <= domain.local[0]; ++x) {
+                const size_t globalX = domain.offset[0] + x - 1;
+                const size_t linearId =
+                    globalZ * (domain.global[0] * domain.global[1]) +
+                    globalY * domain.global[0] + globalX;
+                const double pseudo =
+                    (((linearId + 1) * 1299709) % volume) / static_cast<double>(volume);
+                concentration[domain.index(x, y, z)] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
             }
         }
     }
 }

+bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& concentration, const Domain& domain) {
+    int localFinite = 1;
+    double localMinimum = std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
+    double localMaximum = -std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
+    for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain.local[2]; ++z) {

+std::vector<double> gatherGlobalResult(const std::vector<double>& localField,
+                                       const Domain& domain) {
+    if (domain.ownedSize() > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        if (domain.rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "Error: local result exceeds MPI_Gatherv count limit\n");
+        MPI_Abort(domain.comm, 2);
+    }
+    const int localCount = static_cast<int>(domain.ownedSize());
+    std::vector<double> packed(domain.ownedSize());
+    size_t p = 0;
+    for (size_t z = 1; z <= domain.local[2]; ++z)
+        for (size_t y = 1; y <= domain.local[1]; ++y) {
+            std::copy_n(localField.data() + domain.index(1, y, z), domain.local[0],
+                        packed.data() + p);
+            p += domain.local[0];
+        }
+
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    if (domain.rank == 0) {
+        counts.resize(domain.processCount);
+        displacements.resize(domain.processCount);
+    }
+    MPI_Gather(&localCount, 1, MPI_INT, counts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, 0, domain.comm);
+
+    std::vector<double> rankOrdered;
+    if (domain.rank == 0) {
+        long long total = 0;
+        for (int r = 0; r < domain.processCount; ++r) {
+            if (total > std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) {
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Error: global result exceeds MPI_Gatherv displacement limit\n");
+                MPI_Abort(domain.comm, 2);
+            }
+            displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(total);
+            total += counts[r];
+        }
+        if (total > std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "Error: global result exceeds MPI_Gatherv count limit\n");
+            MPI_Abort(domain.comm, 2);
+        }
+        rankOrdered.resize(static_cast<size_t>(total));
+    }
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(packed.data(), localCount, MPI_DOUBLE, rankOrdered.data(), counts.data(),
+                displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, domain.comm);
+
+    std::vector<double> globalField;
+    if (domain.rank == 0) {
+        globalField.resize(domain.global[0] * domain.global[1] * domain.global[2]);
+        for (int r = 0; r < domain.processCount; ++r) {
+            std::array<int, 3> coordinates{};
+            MPI_Cart_coords(domain.comm, r, 3, coordinates.data());
+            const Extent ex = splitExtent(domain.global[0], domain.dims[0], coordinates[0]);
+            const Extent ey = splitExtent(domain.global[1], domain.dims[1], coordinates[1]);
+            const Extent ez = splitExtent(domain.global[2], domain.dims[2], coordinates[2]);
+            size_t source = static_cast<size_t>(displacements[r]);
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < ez.size; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < ey.size; ++y) {
+                    const size_t destination =
+                        idx3(ex.offset, ey.offset + y, ez.offset + z, domain.global[0],
+                             domain.global[1]);
+                    std::copy_n(rankOrdered.data() + source, ex.size,
+                                globalField.data() + destination);
+                    source += ex.size;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return globalField;
+}

Implemented unconditional MPI distributed-memory parallelism while preserving the `cahn_hilliard` executable name.

Key changes:

- 3D Cartesian domain decomposition with load-aware process-grid selection.
- Persistent nonblocking halo exchanges in all six directions.
- Communication/computation overlap using separate interior and boundary kernels.
- Distributed initialization and validation.
- Global result reconstruction on rank 0 only for `-r`.
- MPI-aware timing using the slowest rank.
- Required MPI integration in [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/cahn-hilliard_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r4/cahn-hilliard/CMakeLists.txt:8).
- Main implementation in [cahn_hilliard.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/cahn-hilliard_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r4/cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp:39).

Validation completed:

- Bitwise-identical output to the original across 1–12 ranks and uneven grids.
- Default result hash: `945edb7faf25aaee`.
- AddressSanitizer and UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer passed.
- Local default-case performance measured approximately 319 MCellUpdates/s on one rank and 749 MCellUpdates/s on four ranks.
